				<script>
					max_height = 0;
					$('.view_cell .event .title').each(function () {
						$this_height = $(this).height();
            //console.log($this_height);
						if ( $this_height >= 40 ) {
							$(this).addClass('shortened');
						}
						if ( $this_height > max_height ) {
							max_height = $this_height;
						}
					});
					$('.view_cell .set_height').css({ 'height' : '50px' });
					$('.mansarda_only, .the_beat_only, .main_scene_only, .myasnitskaya_only, .event.sort-genre-deactive').remove();

setDatepicker(10, 2026, 'ru');

function setDatepicker (curMonth, curYear, language) {

	$.ajax({
		url: "/wp-content/themes/KozlovCLub/components/datepicker.php",
		method: 'GET',
		data : {
			'month' : curMonth,
			'year' : curYear,
			'language': language,
		}
	}).done(function (data) {
		data = jQuery.parseJSON(data);
		$('.calendar-picker-content').html(data);
	});
}

$(document).on('click', '.datepicker_nav-item', function () {
	var setMonth = $(this).data('set-month');
	var setYear = $(this).data('set-year');
	var language = 'ru';

	$.ajax({
		url: "/wp-content/themes/KozlovCLub/components/datepicker.php",
		method: 'GET',
		data : {
			'month': setMonth,
			'year': setYear,
			'language': language,
		}
	}).done(function (data) {
		data = jQuery.parseJSON(data);
		$('.calendar-picker-content').html(data);
	});
});

$(document).on('click', '.block-modal-close', function () {
	$(this).parents('.block-modal').fadeOut();
});

$(document).on('click', '.calendar-toggler', function (e) {
  e.preventDefault();
	$('.block-modal--datepicker').fadeIn();
});

</script>
